  summary: 'Deadline date : 31/12/2028 By European Urban Initiative An ongoing Call
    for the role of peer reviewer is now open. This call plans to identify the urban
    authority representatives to join the pool of peers and to act as peer reviewers
    in future Peer Reviews. Peer reviewers apply as individuals to take part in a
    peer review process. Peer reviewers can represent any EU urban authority but must

  partnership_requirements: Confirm partnership requirements in the official call.
  other_requirements: Open · &nbsp; grant Funding support :&nbsp; Around €1-2 million
    Find out more
  summary: 'Deadline date : 17/11/2026 By Driving Urban Transitions to a Sustainable
    future The Driving Urban Transition (DUT) Partnership has announced its fourth
    call for proposals, DUT Call 2026, which opens for applications on 1 September.
    This call seeks innovative projects that accelerate the transition to climate
    neutral and resilient cities. The aim of this call for proposals is to support
    transnational research and innovation projects that help cities tackle urban challenges
    and transition towards a climate neutral and resilient future for all. Research
    organisations, companies and commercial organisations, urban government authorities,
    consumers and civil society representatives and actors from the culture and creative
    sectors.'

    1 September. This call seeks innovative projects that accelerate the transition
    to climate neutral and resilient cities. The aim of this call for proposals is
